   Appearance

   From Wikipedia, the free encyclopedia
   (Redirected from [172]1000-1999 (numbers))

   "1,000", "Thousand", and "Chiliad" redirect here. For other uses, see
   [173]1000 (disambiguation) or, for the military unit, see
   [174]Chiliarchy.

   Natural number
   [175]← 999 1000 [176]1001 →
     * [177]List of numbers
     * [178]Integers

   [179]← [180]0 1k [181]2k [182]3k [183]4k [184]5k [185]6k [186]7k
   [187]8k [188]9k [189]→
   [190]Cardinal one thousand
   [191]Ordinal 1000th
   (one thousandth)
   [192]Factorization 2^3 × 5^3
   [193]Divisors 1, 2, 4, 5, 8, 10, 20, 25, 40, 50, 100, 125, 200, 250,
   500, 1000
   [194]Greek numeral ,Α´

   [197]Unicode symbol(s) ↀ

   [201]Latin [202]prefix [203]milli

   [204]Binary 1111101000[2]
   [205]Ternary 1101001[3]
   [206]Senary 4344[6]
   [207]Octal 1750[8]
   [208]Duodecimal 6B4[12]
   [209]Hexadecimal 3E8[16]
   [210]Tamil ௲
   [211]Chinese 千

   Look up [216]thousand or [217]1000 in Wiktionary, the free dictionary.

   1000 or one thousand is the [218]natural number following [219]999 and
   preceding [220]1001. In most [221]English-speaking countries, it can be
   written with or without a comma or sometimes a period [222]separating
   the thousands digit: 1,000.

   A group of one thousand things is sometimes known, from [223]Ancient
   Greek, as a chiliad.^[224][1] A period of one thousand years may be
   known as a chiliad or, more often from [225]Latin, as a
   [226]millennium. The number 1000 is also sometimes described as a short
   thousand in medieval contexts where it is necessary to distinguish the
   [227]Germanic concept of 1200 as a [228]long thousand. It is the first
   4-digit [229]integer.

Notation

   [[230]edit]

     * The decimal representation for one thousand is
          + 1000—a [231]one followed by three [232]zeros, in the general
            notation;
          + 1 × 10^3—in [233]engineering notation, which for this number
            coincides with:
          + 1 × 10^3 exactly—in [234]scientific normalized exponential
            notation;
          + 1 E+3 exactly—in [235]scientific E notation.
     * The [236]SI prefix for a thousand units is "[237]kilo-",
       abbreviated to "k"—for instance, a [238]kilogram or "kg" is a
       thousand [239]grams. This is sometimes extended to non-SI contexts,
       such as "ka" ([240]kiloannum) being used as a shorthand for periods
       of 1000 years. In [241]computer science, however, "kilo-" is used
       more loosely to mean 2 to the 10th power (1024).
     * In the [242]SI writing style, a [243]non-breaking space can be used
       as a [244]thousands separator, i.e., to separate the digits of a
       number at every power of 1000.
     * Multiples of thousands are occasionally represented by replacing
       their last three zeros with the letter "K" or "k": for instance,
       writing "$30k" for $30 000 or denoting the [245]Y2K computer bug of
       the year 2000.
     * A thousand units of [246]currency, especially [247]dollars or
       [248]pounds, are colloquially called a grand. In the United States,
       this is sometimes abbreviated with a "G" suffix.

In Mathematics

   [[249]edit]

   A [250]chiliagon is a 1000-sided [251]polygon.^[252][2]

Numbers in the range 1001–1999

   [[253]edit]

1001 to 1099

   [[254]edit]

     * [255]1001 = [256]sphenic number (7 × 11 × 13), [257]pentagonal
       number, [258]pentatope number, [259]palindromic number
     * 1002 = sphenic number, [260]Mertens function zero, [261]abundant
       number, number of [262]partitions of 22
     * 1003 = the product of some prime p and the p^th prime, namely p =
       17.
     * 1004 = [263]heptanacci number^[264][3]
     * 1005 = Mertens function zero, decagonal pyramidal number^[265][4]
     * 1006 = [266]semiprime, product of two distinct [267]isolated primes
       (2 and 503); [268]unusual number; [269]square-free number; number
       of [270]compositions (ordered [271]partitions) of 22 into squares;
       sum of two distinct [272]pentatope numbers (5 and 1001); number of
       undirected [273]Hamiltonian paths in 4 by 5 [274]square grid
       graph;^[275][5] record gap between [276]twin primes;^[277][6]
       number that is the sum of 7 positive 5th powers.^[278][7] In
       decimal: [279]equidigital number; when turned around, the number
       looks like a prime, 9001; its [280]cube can be [281]concatenated
       from other cubes, 1_0_1_8_1_0_8_216 ("_" indicates concatenation, 0
       = 0^3, 1 = 1^3, 8 = 2^3, 216 = 6^3)^[282][8]
     * 1007 = number that is the sum of 8 positive 5th powers^[283][9]
     * 1008 = divisible by the number of primes below it
     * 1009 = smallest four-digit [284]prime, [285]palindromic in bases
       11, 15, 19, 24 and 28: (838[11], 474[15], 2F2[19], 1I1[24],
       181[28]). It is also a [286]Lucky prime and [287]Chen prime.
     * 1010 = 10^3 + 10,^[288][10] Mertens function zero
     * 1011 = the largest n such that 2^n contains 101 and does not
       contain 11011, [289]Harshad number in bases 5, 10, 15, 20, 25, 30,
       35, 40, 45, 50, 55, 60, 65, 70, 75 (and 202 other bases), number of
       partitions of 1 into reciprocals of positive integers <= 16
       [290]Egyptian fraction^[291][11]
     * 1012 = [292]ternary number, (32[10]) quadruple [293]triangular
       number ([294]triangular number is [295]253),^[296][12] number of
       partitions of 1 into reciprocals of positive integers <= 17
       [297]Egyptian fraction^[298][11]
     * 1013 = [299]Sophie Germain prime,^[300][13] [301]centered square
       number,^[302][14] Mertens function zero
     * 1014 = 2^10-10,^[303][15] Mertens function zero, sum of the
       nontriangular numbers between successive triangular numbers 78 and
       91^[304][16]
     * 1015 = [305]square pyramidal number^[306][17]
     * 1016 = member of the [307]Mian–Chowla sequence,^[308][18]
       [309]stella octangula number, number of surface points on a cube
       with edge-length 14^[310][19]
     * 1017 = generalized triacontagonal number^[311][20]
     * 1018 = Mertens function zero, 1018^16 + 1 is prime^[312][21]
     * 1019 = [313]Sophie Germain prime,^[314][13] [315]safe
       prime,^[316][22] [317]Chen prime
     * 1020 = [318]polydivisible number
     * 1021 = [319]twin prime with 1019. It is also a [320]Lucky prime.
     * 1022 = [321]Friedman number
     * [322]1023 = sum of five consecutive primes (193 + 197 + 199 + 211 +
       223);^[323][23] the number of [324]three-dimensional [325]polycubes
       with [326]7 cells;^[327][24] number of [328]elements in a
       [329]9-simplex; highest number one can count to on one's fingers
       using binary; magic number used in [330]Global Positioning System
       signals.
     * [331]1024 = 32^2 = 4^5 = 2^10, the number of [332]bytes in a
       [333]kilobyte (in 1999, the [334]IEC coined [335]kibibyte to use
       for 1024 with kilobyte being 1000, but this convention has not been
       widely adopted). 1024 is the smallest 4-digit square and also a
       [336]Friedman number.
     * 1025 = [337]Proth number 2^10 + 1; member of [338]Moser–de Bruijn
       sequence, because its base-4 representation (100001[4]) contains
       only digits 0 and 1, or it's a sum of distinct powers of 4 (4^5 +
       4^0); [339]Jacobsthal-Lucas number; hypotenuse of [340]primitive
       Pythagorean triangle
     * 1026 = sum of two distinct powers of 2 ([341]1024 + [342]2)
     * 1027 = sum of the squares of the first eight primes; can be written
       from base 2 to base 18 using only the digits 0 to 9.
     * 1028 = sum of totient function for first 58 integers; can be
       written from base 2 to base 18 using only the digits 0 to 9; number
       of primes <= 2^13.^[343][25]
     * 1029 = can be written from base 2 to base 18 using only the digits
       0 to 9.
     * 1030 = generalized heptagonal number
     * 1031 = exponent and number of ones for the fifth base-10
       [344]repunit prime,^[345][26] [346]Sophie Germain prime,^[347][13]
       [348]super-prime, [349]Chen prime
     * 1032 = sum of two distinct powers of 2 ([350]1024 + [351]8)
     * 1033 = [352]emirp, [353]twin prime with 1031
     * 1034 = sum of 12 positive 9th powers^[354][27]
     * 1035 = 45th [355]triangular number,^[356][28] [357]hexagonal
       number^[358][29]
     * 1036 = central polygonal number^[359][30]
     * 1037 = number in E-toothpick sequence^[360][31]
     * 1038 = [361]even [362]integer that is an unordered sum of two
       [363]primes in exactly n ways^[364][32]
     * 1039 = [365]prime of the form 8n+7,^[366][33] number of partitions
       of 30 that do not contain 1 as a part,^[367][34] [368]Chen prime,
       [369]Lucky prime
     * 1040 = 4^5 + 4^2: sum of distinct powers of 4.^[370][35] The number
       of pieces that could be seen in a 6 × 6 × 6× 6 Rubik's Tesseract.
     * 1041 = sum of 11 positive 5th powers^[371][36]
     * 1042 = sum of 12 positive 5th powers^[372][37]
     * 1043 = number whose sum of [373]even digits and sum of [374]odd
       digits are even^[375][38]
     * 1044 = sum of distinct powers of 4^[376][35]
     * 1045 = [377]octagonal number^[378][39]
     * 1046 = coefficient of f(q) (3rd order mock theta
       function)^[379][40]
     * 1047 = number of ways to split a strict composition of 18 into
       contiguous subsequences that have the same sum^[380][41]
     * 1048 = number of partitions of 27 into [381]squarefree
       parts^[382][42]
     * 1049 = [383]Sophie Germain prime,^[384][13] [385]highly cototient
       number,^[386][43] [387]Chen prime
     * 1050 = 1050[8] to [388]decimal becomes a [389]pronic number
       (552[10]),^[390][44] number of parts in all partitions of 29 into
       distinct parts^[391][45]
     * 1051 = [392]centered pentagonal number,^[393][46] [394]centered
       decagonal number
     * 1052 = sum of 9 positive 6th powers^[395][47]
     * 1053 = triangular matchstick number^[396][48]
     * 1054 = [397]centered triangular number^[398][49]
     * 1055 = sum of 12 positive 6th powers^[399][50]
     * 1056 = [400]pronic number^[401][51]
     * 1057 = central polygonal number^[402][52]
     * 1058 = sum of 4 positive 5th powers,^[403][53] area of a square
       with diagonal 46^[404][54]
     * 1059 = number n such that n^4 is written in the form of a sum of
       four positive 4th powers^[405][55]
     * 1060 = sum of the first twenty-five primes from [406]2 through
       [407]97 (the number of primes less than [408]100),^[409][56] and
       sixth sum of 10 consecutive primes, starting with [410]23 through
       [411]131.^[412][57]
     * 1061 = [413]emirp, [414]twin prime with 1063, number of prime
       numbers between 1000 and [415]10000 (or, number of four-digit
       primes in [416]decimal representation)^[417][58]
     * 1062 = number that is not the sum of two [418]palindromes^[419][59]
     * 1063 = [420]super-prime, sum of seven consecutive primes (137 + 139
       + 149 + 151 + 157 + 163 + 167); near-wall-sun-sun prime^[421][60].
       It is also a [422]twin prime with 1061.
     * 1064 = sum of two positive [423]cubes^[424][61]
     * 1065 = generalized duodecagonal^[425][62]
     * 1066 = number whose sum of their [426]divisors is a
       [427]square^[428][63]
     * 1067 = number of strict [429]integer [430]partitions of 45 in which
       are empty or have smallest [431]part not dividing the other
       ones^[432][64]
     * 1068 = number that is the sum of 7 positive 5th powers,^[433][7]
       total number of parts in all partitions of 15^[434][65]
     * 1069 = [435]emirp^[436][66]
     * 1070 = number that is the sum of 9 positive 5th powers^[437][67]
     * 1071 = [438]heptagonal number^[439][68]
     * 1072 = [440]centered heptagonal number^[441][69]
     * 1073 = number that is the sum of 12 positive 5th powers^[442][37]
     * 1074 = number that is not the sum of two [443]palindromes^[444][59]
     * 1075 = number non-sum of two [445]palindromes^[446][59]
     * 1076 = number of strict trees weight 11^[447][70]
     * 1077 = number where [448]7 outnumbers every other digit in the
       number^[449][71]
     * 1078 = [450]Euler transform of negative [451]integers^[452][72]
     * 1079 = every positive integer is the sum of at most 1079 tenth
       powers.
     * 1080 = pentagonal number,^[453][73] [454]largely composite
       number^[455][74]
     * 1081 = 46th [456]triangular number,^[457][28] member of
       [458]Padovan sequence^[459][75]
     * 1082 = central polygonal number^[460][30]
     * 1083 = three-quarter [461]square,^[462][76] number of partitions of
       53 into prime parts^[463][77]
     * 1084 = [464]third spoke of a hexagonal spiral,^[465][78]
       [466]1084^64 + 1 is prime
     * 1085 = number of partitions of n into distinct [467]parts > or =
       [468]2^[469][79]
     * 1086 = [470]Smith number,^[471][80] sum of totient function for
       first 59 integers
     * 1087 = super-prime, [472]cousin prime, [473]lucky prime^[474][81]
     * 1088 = octo-[475]triangular number, ([476]triangular number result
       being [477]136)^[478][82] sum of two distinct powers of 2,
       ([479]1024 + [480]64)^[481][83] number that is divisible by exactly
       seven [482]primes with the inclusion of [483]multiplicity^[484][84]
     * [485]1089 = 33^2, [486]nonagonal number, [487]centered octagonal
       number, first natural number whose digits in its decimal
       representation get reversed when multiplied by 9.^[488][85]
     * 1090 = sum of 5 positive 5th powers^[489][86]
     * 1091 = [490]cousin prime and [491]twin prime with 1093
     * 1092 = divisible by the number of primes below it
     * [492]1093 = the smallest [493]Wieferich prime (the only other known
       [494]Wieferich prime is 3511^[495][87]), [496]twin prime with 1091
       and [497]star number^[498][88]
     * 1094 = sum of 9 positive 5th powers,^[499][67] [500]1094^64 + 1 is
       prime
     * 1095 = sum of 10 positive 5th powers,^[501][89] number that is not
       the sum of two [502]palindromes
     * 1096 = hendecagonal number,^[503][90] number of strict solid
       partitions of 18^[504][91]
     * 1097 = [505]emirp,^[506][66] [507]Chen prime
     * 1098 = multiple of 9 containing digit 9 in its [508]base-10
       representation^[509][92]
     * 1099 = number where 9 outnumbers every other digit^[510][93]

1100 to 1199

   [[511]edit]

     * 1100 = number of partitions of 61 into distinct squarefree
       parts^[512][94]
     * 1101 = pinwheel number^[513][95]
     * 1102 = sum of totient function for first 60 integers
     * 1103 = [514]Sophie Germain prime,^[515][13] [516]balanced
       prime^[517][96]
     * 1104 = [518]Keith number^[519][97]
     * [520]1105 = 33^2 + 4^2 = 32^2 + 9^2 = 31^2 + 12^2 = 23^2 + 24^2,
       [521]Carmichael number,^[522][98] [523]magic constant of n × n
       normal [524]magic square and [525]n-queens problem for n = 13,
       [526]decagonal number,^[527][99] centered square number,^[528][14]
       [529]Fermat pseudoprime^[530][100]
     * 1106 = number of regions into which the plane is divided when
       drawing 24 ellipses^[531][101]
     * 1107 = number of non-isomorphic strict T[0] multiset partitions of
       weight 8^[532][102]
     * 1108 = [533]number k such that k^64 + 1 is prime
     * 1109 = Friedlander-Iwaniec prime,^[534][103] [535]Chen prime
     * 1110 = k such that 2^k + 3 is prime^[536][104]
     * 1111 = 11 × 101, palindrome that is a product of two palindromic
       primes,^[537][105] [538]repunit^[539][106]
     * 1112 = k such that 9^k - 2 is a prime^[540][107]
     * 1113 = number of strict partions of 40^[541][108]
     * 1114 = number of ways to write 22 as an orderless product of
       orderless sums^[542][109]
     * 1115 = number of partitions of 27 into a prime number of
       parts^[543][110]
     * 1116 = divisible by the number of primes below it
     * 1117 = number of diagonally symmetric polyominoes with 16
       cells,^[544][111] [545]Chen prime
     * 1118 = number of unimodular 2 × 2 matrices having all terms in
       {0,1,...,21}^[546][112]
     * 1119 = number of [547]bipartite graphs with 9 nodes^[548][113]
     * 1120 = [549]number k such that k^64 + 1 is prime
     * 1121 = number of squares between 34^2 and 34^4.^[550][114]
     * 1122 = pronic number,^[551][51] divisible by the number of primes
       below it
     * 1123 = [552]balanced prime^[553][96]
     * 1124 = [554]Leyland number^[555][115] using 2 & 10 (2^10 + 10^2),
       spy number
     * 1125 = [556]Achilles number
     * 1126 = number of 2 × 2 non-singular integer matrices with entries
       from {0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5}^[557][116]
     * 1127 = maximal number of pieces that can be obtained by cutting an
       annulus with 46 cuts^[558][117]
     * 1128 = 47th [559]triangular number,^[560][28] 24th hexagonal
       number,^[561][29] divisible by the number of primes below it

Prime numbers

   [[1747]edit]

   2000:^[1749][510]^[1750][511]

          1009, 1013, 1019, 1021, 1031, 1033, 1039, 1049, 1051, 1061,
